## 9. X3 — Science KS1–KS4 extension: 155 more verified pictures (biology, chemistry, physics, earth and space)

Author: Agent X3 (2026-09-20). Owner's words: "every page needs an appropriate image suited to what it is you are talking about … ITS INCREDIBLY IMPORTANT THAT THE IMAGE DOESNT CONTRADICT THE PAGE OR CONTENTS SO DO YOUR RESEARCH FOR EVERY IMAGE." Policy unchanged (a picture only when a verified match exists); this section raises the verified coverage of Science (Biology, Chemistry, Physics, Combined Science and KS1-3 Science: 1,498 lessons, 11,443 generated slides).

### 9.1 Measurement (before)
* 11,443 Science slides; 7,880 are teaching slides (not question slides); **540 (4.7%) had a library picture** (236 of 1,498 lessons), 123 had a literal emoji; only 31 different pictures were ever used (`changes-of-state` 111, `food-chain` 54, `photosynthesis` 59, `plant-parts` 36 …).
* Concepts named in a title / key-word term / lead of a teaching slide that had NO picture (slides affected; whole-phrase match, before X3): force 218, electron 141, current 136, speed 122, DNA 118, forces 113, charge 110, acceleration 109, reactant 108, resistance 100, blood 88, radiation 87, enzyme 82, atmosphere 81, frequency 80, life cycle 74, heart 73, gene 73, pressure 72, microscope 71, hormone 68, rate of reaction 66, greenhouse gas 65, allele 62, genome 54, variation 54, density 52, phenotype 49, pathogen 49, seed 48, electromagnet 48, diffusion 47, osmosis 44, soluble 44, distillation 43, shadow 41, natural selection 40, electrolysis 40, velocity–time graph 38, isotope 36, dissolve 34 …
* Why the 36 existing Science pictures did not fire (rejected-candidate reasons over the Science decks, count of slides): the picture needed a context word the slide lacked (`atom` needs nucleus/electron/... 241, `changes-of-state` 104), an `avoid` phrase vetoed it (`plant-parts` 146, `atom` 129, `simple-circuit` 119, `transverse-wave` 80, `respiration` 71), not prominent enough (`changes-of-state` 98, `plant-parts` 70, `states-of-matter` 51, `simple-circuit` 46, `photosynthesis` 45) and, above all, **no picture existed** for hearts, DNA, enzymes, apparatus, bonding, forces, energy, light, sound, radioactivity …

### 9.2 What was added
`server/src/oak/factory/art/ext-science-{kit,bio1,bio2,bio3,chem1,chem2,phys}.ts` (+ `ext-science.ts` = the list `EXT_SCIENCE`, + `ext-science-tests.ts`), registered by ONE line in `art/library.ts`. 135 hand-drawn generic diagrams + 20 electronic-structure pictures (H to Ca) = **155 pictures**, all `subjects: ["Science"]`:

* **Human body**: skeleton, digestive, breathing system, alveoli, heart, double circulation, blood vessels, blood, nervous system, reflex arc, neurone, eye, ear, kidneys/urinary, sperm and egg cell, female / male reproductive system, teeth, tooth structure, endocrine system, homeostasis (negative feedback), antagonistic muscles, balanced diet, pathogens, levels of organisation …
* **Plants and life cycles**: leaf structure, stomata, root hair cell, xylem and phloem, transpiration, seed, germination, plant / butterfly / frog life cycles, phototropism.
* **Cells, genetics, classification, ecology**: bacterial and yeast cell, specialised cells, mitosis, DNA double helix, chromosome-gene-DNA, Punnett square, alleles/genotype/phenotype, natural selection, vertebrate groups, invertebrates, classification key, five kingdoms, taxonomy, pyramid of biomass, carbon cycle, predator-prey, quadrat, enzyme (lock and key + denatured), diffusion, osmosis, active transport, light microscope.
* **Chemistry**: glassware, Bunsen burner/tripod/gauze, meniscus, titration, soluble/insoluble, filtration, simple distillation, crystallisation, paper chromatography, protons/neutrons/electrons, atomic and mass number, isotopes, sodium chloride ionic bonding, ionic lattice, covalent bonds (H2, H2O), diamond and graphite, metallic bonding, metals and non-metals, reactivity series, conservation of mass, combustion + fire triangle, neutralisation, reactions of acids, electrolysis, rate-of-reaction graph, catalyst profile, composition of the atmosphere, greenhouse effect, rock cycle, three rock types; **electronic structure of the first 20 elements** (a picture appears only when the slide names that element's atom / electronic structure).
* **Earth and space**: seasons (axis tilt), day and night, solar and lunar eclipses, orbits, life cycle of a star, gravity.
* **Physics**: formula triangles (speed, density, pressure, acceleration, wave equation, V = I R), ammeter/voltmeter circuit, I-V graphs, electromagnet, magnetic field of a bar magnet, like/unlike charges, energy stores, Sankey diagram, renewable/non-renewable resources, conduction/convection/radiation, moments, Hooke's law, friction/air/water resistance, force arrows, contact vs non-contact forces, terminal velocity, stopping distance, work done, how we see (light source), shadows, transparent/translucent/opaque, prism dispersion, convex and concave lenses, how sound travels, pitch and loudness, frequency and period, alpha/beta/gamma penetration, half-life, fission, fusion, models of the atom, National Grid, a.c./d.c., mains wires (live/neutral/earth colours), electrical conductors and insulators.

Every diagram is **generic-correct**: labels only, no invented numbers, specific-instance pictures carry that instance as a required concept (element atoms; NaCl for ionic bonding; the Bb x Bb Punnett square is marked "an example" and is `numeric`, so it never appears on a slide with digits). Scientific checks made while drawing: arrow directions (food and energy, blood flow, ions to the correct electrode: negative terminal = short thick battery line = cathode; convection: hot fluid rises), left/right anatomy (the person's right on the viewer's left), electron counts and shell fill (2,8,8,...), exact sector angles for the air pie, tilt of the axis fixed in the same direction for both seasons, red bends least in the prism, the pull arrow points away from the box, the wasted-energy band of the Sankey diagram widths add up (14 + 30 = 44), 4 H and 2 O atoms on each side of 2H2 + O2 -> 2H2O.

Ambiguity gates (`requires` / `avoid` / lesson- and unit-title vetoes, `numeric`) are set per picture (ext-science*.ts): e.g. `heart` never on "heart rate"; `respiratory-system` never on cellular respiration; `neurone` not for sensory/relay neurones; `dissolving` not on concentration / moles / titration; `sankey` not on chemical "efficiency"; `electrolysis` not on electric cells; `distillation` not for fractional distillation; `bacterial-cell` not for viruses; `catalyst` not on enzymes; `atmosphere` not for the early atmosphere; `gravity` not on seedlings; `pressure` not on blood/atmospheric/rock pressure; `sound` pictures not on "particles vibrate" (heating) slides; prefix-avoids that would over-veto ("mole" vs "molecule", "tem" vs "temperature") are whole-word (`=mole`).

### 9.3 Results (Science, all 1,498 lessons, generated decks; `npx tsx src/oak/factory/cli.ts check`: all 819 + 289 + 314 + 76 Science decks valid, **0 invalid Science lessons**)

| | Before | After X3 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Science slides with a verified library picture | 540 of 11,443 (**4.7%**) | **2,632 (23.0%)** |
| ... as a share of teaching (non-question) slides (7,880) | 6.9% | **33.4%** |
| Lessons with at least one picture | 236 of 1,498 (15.8%) | **904 (60.4%)** |
| Different pictures used on Science slides | 31 | 159 (128 of the 155 X3 pictures are used; the other 27 are 19 element atoms that need "<element> atom" in the title/lead, plus eclipses, fission, a.c./d.c., magnetic field of a bar magnet (vetoed on electromagnet lessons), seed, frog cycle, root hair, levels of organisation) |
| By stage (slides with a picture / all slides) | Bio KS4 31/1,226; Chem KS4 29/1,186; Combined KS4 116/2,789; Phys KS4 62/1,109; KS1 29/570; KS2 163/2,331; KS3 110/2,232 | Bio KS4 350; Chem KS4 220; Combined KS4 740; Phys KS4 287; KS1 58; KS2 461; KS3 516 |
| Slides with a literal emoji | 123 | 113 (a picture takes the slot) |
| Practice / check / question slides with a picture | 0 | 0 (unchanged; asserted for every picture by the tests) |

Most used X3 pictures: DNA 73, chromosome/gene/genome 72, enzyme 69, V = I R 69, greenhouse effect 65, alleles 61, dissolving 52, bacterial cell 50, Sankey 50, electrolysis 46, rate graph 46, endocrine 42, microscope 42, friction 40, shadows 39 …

### 9.4 Mistakes found by reading real slides, and fixed (audit of ~10 sample slides per picture over the real decks)
`dissolving` appeared on "Solutions: exchange surfaces", "Concentration of solutions", red-cabbage indicator solution (bare `solution` concept removed; concentration/moles/titration/indicator now veto); `sankey` on chemical equilibrium "efficiency, safety and cost"; `electrolysis` on "Electric cells and batteries"; `balanced-diet` on phloem and egg-cell slides (bare `nutrient` now needs diet context, plant/cell words veto); `sound-travel` on "heating a solid makes its particles vibrate" and electronic music (bare `sound`/`vibrate` removed); `forces-arrows` on gears and weathering key words (bare `force` removed); `levels-organisation` on "potato tissue" (bare `tissue`/`organ` removed); `gravity` on seedling gravitropism; `energy-stores` on ionising radiation; `energy-resources` on fuel cells; `ionic-bonding` on anion tests; `titration` on an enzyme "end point"; `speed` on satellites and parachutes (bare `speed` removed); `measuring-electricity` on electric shock / high voltage; `seeing-light` on the inverse-square law; `skeleton` on "animal skeletons"; `hookes-law` on the energy of a spring; `rate-graph` on chemical equilibrium; plus the fixes to the drawings themselves (the pull arrow direction, the battery polarity, the number of water molecules in 2H2 + O2, wasted-energy band, cathode/anode labels, overlapping labels).
Remaining known limits: on multi-term "Key words" slides a picture shows for ONE of the terms (e.g. a DNA picture on the animal-cell key words) - correct, never contradicting, but tangential; the generic picture cannot know a slide's specific example.

### 9.5 Verification
```
cd server
npx tsx src/oak/factory/art/cli.ts check      # 741 pictures, ..., 883 X3 Science tests, 0 problems
npx tsx src/oak/factory/cli.ts check          # 7,470 lessons; Science: INVALID 0 (the run also flags ONE Maths KS4 lesson, "the laws of indices: fractional exponents", for leaked markup "√a = a^(1/2)" in the slide text: not a picture problem, not X3)
npx tsc --noEmit                              # server: only the pre-existing src/curriculum/english-ks2/_check_e2.ts error; root: clean
```
* `ext-science-tests.ts` (883 tests, wired into `art/cli.ts check`): for EVERY one of the 155 pictures a positive slide that must get exactly that picture, a passing-mention negative (must not), a question slide (`practice`, `check`, choice block: must not), the same text in a Maths lesson (must not), `numeric` pictures refused with digits, plus 105 specific negatives (85 hand-written + one "ion, not atom" negative per element) for the ambiguity gates (heart rate, cellular respiration, sensory neurone, compound eye, kidney beans, molar mass, incomplete combustion, fractional distillation, electric cells, early atmosphere, momentum, electrical conduction, ...). Two existing F1 tests were updated because a better answer now exists (nerve cells -> neurone diagram; egg cells -> sperm and egg diagram).
* Contact sheets viewed in light AND dark (all 155 pictures, several rounds; label overlap/overflow is checked by a text-bounds script and by eye; the SVGs use the shared theme classes and inherit light/dark from the page).
* 72 real generated slides (title + real slide text + the picture panel exactly as SlideArt renders it: inline SVG, alt text, caption) were rendered in 4 sheets (2 dark, 2 light) from the real decks of 38 Oak lessons and READ one by one: 67 different pictures, no picture contradicts its slide; tangential cases are listed in 9.4.
* **Staging**: only the 38 lessons of the sample were re-imported (`npx tsx src/oak/import.ts pnH8zTuvYlb7yJbvcanr --slides --lesson <slug>`; not the whole library; never a real tenant). `e2e/x3-science-art-sheet.spec.ts` (a copy of the F1 spec reading `scratch/x3-art-samples.json`, writing `scratch/x3-art-shots*/`) asserts on the real app that each sampled slide renders exactly its pictures (ids, alt text, inline SVG, no empty panel, no horizontal overflow). Run it only via `scripts/e2e-locked.sh e2e/x3-science-art-sheet.spec.ts` (X3_THEME=light|dark, X3_WIDTH=390, X3_LIMIT=n). NOT RUN in the real app by X3: while X3 finished, the dev web server on :3000 (PID 99267, already running) answered 404 for /login, /signup and every route (a stale route table after a build) and Playwright's webServer could not start another one, so the slide review above used the standalone harness instead.

### 9.6 Files
New: `server/src/oak/factory/art/ext-science.ts`, `ext-science-kit.ts`, `ext-science-bio1.ts`, `ext-science-bio2.ts`, `ext-science-bio3.ts`, `ext-science-chem1.ts`, `ext-science-chem2.ts`, `ext-science-phys.ts`, `ext-science-tests.ts`, `e2e/x3-science-art-sheet.spec.ts`, `scratch/x3-art-samples.json`.

### 9.7 Verification table (X3 pictures)

Concepts are exact whole words / phrases (plural of the last word also matches). "Oak evidence" = a keyword definition found in `scratch/oak-raw` for one of the concepts (Biology / Chemistry / Physics / Combined Science / Science programmes); when there is none the picture is drawn from standard curriculum facts, stated in the verification text.

## 10. X1 — Maths KS1-KS2 extension (82 new pictures, 2026-09-20)

Owner's words: "every page needs an appropriate image suited to what it is you are talking about … ITS INCREDIBLY IMPORTANT THAT THE IMAGE DOESNT CONTRADICT THE PAGE OR CONTENTS SO DO YOUR RESEARCH FOR EVERY IMAGE." Agent X1 raised verified coverage for Maths Years 1-6 (Oak `maths-primary-ks1` + `maths-primary-ks2`, 1,072 lessons, 6,931 generated slides) from 13.7% to 43.1% of all slides (55% of the non-question slides) without lowering the bar: every picture is a *generic* diagram of a concept (a structure, a layout, a definition or fixed facts) that cannot contradict a slide about that concept.

### 10.1 Measurement (before)
Method: `scratch/x1/measure.ts / cand.ts` over the generated decks (`cli.ts check --subject Maths --keystage ks1|ks2 --json`), counting whole-phrase concepts in the title / key-word term / lead of every teaching slide that had NO picture. Top uncovered concepts before this work (slides where the concept is prominent, upper bound): part/whole 260, multiple(s) 222, factor/product 132, equation 125, difference/minuend/subtrahend 124, dividend/divisor/quotient/remainder 123, addend/sum 119, regroup 109, greater/less than 85, double/halve 73, times tables 65, coins 59, column addition 56, bridging a ten 53, counting forwards/backwards 52, length units 50, skip counting 45, equal/unequal parts 44, mass units 43, tens and ones 42, one more/less 40, zero 38, divisibility 36, sharing/grouping 35 ... (the rest is in the table below). Reasons a picture was REJECTED for a slide in the final policy run (slides with at least one rejected X1 candidate: 3,962): only mentioned in passing 2,843; vetoed by an `avoid` phrase (other meaning / other topic, e.g. "multiple groups", "sum of angles", "scale factor", "square centimetres", "without crossing", "less than zero") 1,686; numeric picture but the slide has digits 341; polysemous word without its context word (`requires`) 405.

### 10.2 Results (Maths KS1 + KS2, generator output, `cli.ts check` = 0 invalid across all 7,470 lessons)
The generator itself changed under me during the run (other agents; the slide count went 6,931 -> 6,443), so there are two honest comparisons:

| | Start of the run (F1 library only) | Final run: without X1 pictures | Final run: with X1 pictures |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Slides with a verified picture | 953 of 6,931 (13.7%) | 899 of 6,443 (14.0%) | **2,776 of 6,443 (43.1%)** |
| ... of the non-question slides (1,397 of 6,443 are questions) | 18.7% | 17.8% | **55.0%** |
| KS1 slides / KS2 slides | 172 of 2,347 (7.3%) / 781 of 4,584 (17.0%) | 159 of 2,161 (7.4%) / 740 of 4,282 (17.3%) | 829 (38.4%) / 1,947 (45.5%) |
| Lessons with at least one picture | 366 of 1,072 (34.1%) | 350 of 1,072 (32.6%) | **885 (82.6%)** |
| KS1 lessons / KS2 lessons | 74 of 360 (20.6%) / 292 of 712 (41.0%) | 69 (19.2%) / 281 (39.5%) | 272 (75.6%) / 613 (86.1%) |
| Slides showing an X1 picture / lessons with one | 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 | 2,059 / 723 |
| Practice / check / interactive slides with a picture | 0 | 0 | 0 (still never) |

("without X1" = the final decks with the X1 pictures removed: it includes what other agents added meanwhile, e.g. the X2 arithmetic-laws / order-of-operations / clockwise pictures, which own those phrases; X1 dropped its own duplicates of them. The 3 Oak lessons decks that are hand-built are excluded from the JSON.)

Top pictures by use: part-and-whole, multiples, division-terms, equation, subtraction-terms, regrouping, multiplication-terms, addition-terms, multiples-of-ten, uk-coins, multiplication-grid, compare-symbols, column-addition, length-units, mass-units, count-forwards-backwards, equal-and-unequal-parts, skip-counting, divisibility-rules, bridging-ten ... (see `scratch/x1/audit.ts <id>` for every slide a picture is used on).

### 10.3 Policy additions (small, surgical)
* `Pic.keyStages` (types.ts) + a gate in `picVeto` and `dropOutOfScope()` (select.ts): every X1 picture is scoped to `ks1`,`ks2`, so a primary diagram never appears on a KS3/KS4 slide that uses the same word; a picture scoped to other key stages also no longer counts as a "named family member" (it used to block the sphere/cone pictures on KS4 slides that say "3D shape").
* Engine fix (select.ts `scan`): a consumed phrase is now replaced by a non-space filler, so a phrase can no longer match ACROSS a consumed span ("counting [forwards and backwards] in multiples" was matching the concept "counting in multiples").
* `art/ext-maths-primary*.ts` (5 files) + `art/ext-maths-primary-tests.ts`; registered by one import + one array entry in `art/library.ts`; the tests are called from `art/cli.ts check`.

### 10.4 Tests
`npx tsx src/oak/factory/art/cli.ts check`: every new picture has (a) a positive test in KS1 and KS2, (b) negatives: the same text on a practice slide, in KS4, and, for `numeric` pictures, with digits in the text, (c) where it has an `avoid`/`requires` gate, a targeted slide about the other meaning of the word (e.g. `multiples` vs "multiple groups", `addition-terms` vs "sum of angles", `zero` vs "zero as a placeholder", `uk-coins` vs "coin toss", `data-table` vs "times table"). 390 X1 tests, 0 problems.

### 10.5 Visual review
* Contact sheets (light and dark, `scratch/x1/sheet.ts`, then re-rendered after every fix): every picture viewed; fixed overlapping labels/captions in ~25 pictures, off-card text, mis-spaced clocks and turns, a stray red 0 in long multiplication, a crossed-out divider in doubling/halving, an unequal "equal" part in whole/parts.
* Rendered slides: 53 lessons (chosen to cover every picture and the busiest ones twice) were imported to the STAGING tenant only (`import.ts pnH8zTuvYlb7yJbvcanr --slides --lesson <slug>`) and played in tutor Preview by `e2e/x1-maths-art-sheet.spec.ts` (through `scripts/e2e-locked.sh`; asserts exact picture ids, alt text, inline SVG, no empty art panel, no overflow; screenshots to `scratch/x1/shots`). Results are in section 10.6.

### 10.6 Rendered-slide results
* **Real app, staging tenant `pnH8zTuvYlb7yJbvcanr`** (tutor Preview, dark theme, 1100 px): 53 lessons were imported with `--slides --lesson <slug>` (0 failures) and `e2e/x1-maths-art-sheet.spec.ts` (through `scripts/e2e-locked.sh`) played 48 of them: **138 slides with X1 pictures rendered and asserted OK** (rendered picture ids == generator ids on every slide, alt text on every picture, inline SVG, no horizontal overflow, 0 page errors). 5 of the 53 lessons did not finish because the app timed out (3 in-lesson `getAttribute` timeouts, 2 `page.goto` timeouts) while the machine was at load average 600-800 (a first attempt even failed at web-server start-up); no assertion failed. Screenshots: `scratch/x1/shots/` (`results.json`).
* Everything was LOOKED at: the contact sheets (light and dark), ~48 of the real screenshots, and text-vs-picture pairing sheets for 125 real slides (`scratch/x1/pairs.ts`: slide title + text next to the picture at the app's panel size).
* Contradictions/mismatches found by eye on real slides and FIXED (generator gates added + a test row each): the bridging-a-ten picture on "Add two-digit numbers WITHOUT crossing the tens boundary"; whole/parts on "whole metres" and on "equal parts / whole numbers"; the >,<,= picture on "numbers greater than and less than ZERO" and on "10 less than"; the missing-part (whole minus part) picture on a PERCENTAGE "unknown part" slide; the factor-pairs-of-12 picture on "a square number has an odd number of factors"; more/fewer on "more than two parts"; fraction-decimal table on "positive or negative fractions"; H T O column layouts on 4-digit slides (layouts now Th H T O); "sharing/grouping" on multiplication key words; digits on every "2 digit" slide (now only the plural "digits"). NOTE: these fixes were made AFTER the staging import, so the staging decks still show the old pairing on those few slides; the lead's final import applies the fixed generator.

For example, 10 tens can…” (Add up to 3 four-digit numbers using co…)<br>*Drawing:* Oak KS1-KS2 regrouping (exchange): 10 ones = 1 ten, 10 tens = 1 hundred, in both directions.
